# Ptolemy, Harmonics, Latin version by Antonio Gogava (Venice, 1562), OCR text (with Aristoxenus and a fragment of Porphyry)

- **Title:** Aristoxeni musici antiquiss. Harmonicorum elementorum libri III. Cl. Ptolemaei Harmonicorum, seu de Musica lib. III. Aristotelis De obiecto auditus fragmentum ex Porphyrii commentariis
- **Author:** Aristoxenus; Claudius Ptolemy; Aristotle (De obiecto auditus, fragment, from Porphyry)
- **Edition:** Venice, apud Vincentium Valgrisium, 1562; all first rendered into Latin and edited by Antonio Gogava (1529-1569); the first printed text of Ptolemy's Harmonics, in Latin. The Greek text was not printed.
- **Translator:** Antonio Gogava (Latin)
- **Year:** 1562
- **Read from:** https://archive.org/details/aristoxenimusici00aris (file aristoxenimusici00aris_djvu.txt); scan PDF kept beside it (gogava-1562-latin-scan.pdf). Another copy: https://archive.org/details/bub_gb_7NrRhOOPV7AC
- **Licence / copyright status:** Public domain: the work is Ptolemy (2nd century); the edition, translation and print are of the date given, and the editor/translator/printer died long before 1955 (worldwide public domain by age). The digitisation carries no claim of its own that was found; the Internet Archive item states it as public domain / Public Domain Mark.
